function notifyMe() {
// Let's check if the browser supports notifications
if (!("Notification" in window)) {
console.log("This browser does not support desktop notification");
}else if (Notification.permission === "granted") { // Let's check whether notification permissions have alredy been granted
appboy.openSession();
appboy.registerAppboyPushMessages();
appboy.requestInAppMessageRefresh();
appboy.display.automaticallyShowNewInAppMessages();
}else if (Notification.permission !== 'denied' || Notification.permission === "default") {  // Otherwise, we need to ask the user for permission
Notification.requestPermission(function (permission) {
// If the user accepts, let's create a notification
if (permission === "granted") {
//var notification = new Notification("Hi there2");
